## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a clear four-part route (Home category, advantages, growth, financials) with consistent purple branding, divider slides, and action-style titles, and it closes on a margin roadmap that the audience can follow (slides 46 and 47). Several slides communicate well, such as the speed badge flow on 16, the CastleGate comparison table on 15, and the stepped profitability slide on 46. Weaknesses are consequential: slide 14 is an overlay that hides the value-chain diagram beneath it, some pages are sparse or low in information (19, 29 to 31, 39), and the two revenue and gross margin charts (42, 43) use small labels and dual series that are hard to read. Slide 47 has a floating gross margin bar with no value, a crowded text panel, and a waterfall that does not reconcile visibly, which undercuts the closing page.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the section structure, the purple title bar system, the badge-lift flow on slide 16, and the three-step roadmap on slide 46.

**Most useful repair:** Fix the overlap on slide 14, give the gross margin bar on slide 47 a label and a value, and enlarge the data labels and legends on slides 42 to 45.

**What would change the score:** I would cut or merge thin pages such as 29 to 31, replace the small screenshot collages on 9 and 12 with one or two clear examples, and add takeaway callouts to the financial charts.

_Confidence medium. 51 of 51 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck is Wayfair's 2025 investor presentation, dated February 20, 2025, and opens with a forward-looking statements safe harbor before a contents page. It then moves through four main sections: the Home category, where the company sizes the market and describes how home shopping differs from search-based retail; competitive advantages, covering brand reach, the CastleGate logistics network, speed-driven conversion, a cost markup model, product range, the Wayfair Verified curation label, a membership program, customer service and generative AI; growth initiatives, including Wayfair Professional, a luxury brand push, international expansion into Canada, the UK and Ireland, physical stores and advertising; and financials, covering revenue and orders, gross margin, fixed cost discipline, capital structure and a stepped path toward 10% adjusted EBITDA margin. An appendix defines the non-GAAP measures used. The deck closes on the margin roadmap and the appendix. Slides worth studying include the speed badge conversion chart, the CastleGate comparison, the Wayfair Verified performance slide, and the multi-step profitability roadmap.

## Questions about this deck

**What market size does the Wayfair investor presentation give for the Home category?**

Slide 6 says the 2025 TAM is >$500B across the US, Canada and the UK, and that Wayfair's 2024 figure is ~$12B. It also states that the home category exceeds $500B in North America and the UK today.

**How many slides does the Wayfair 2025 investor presentation have and how is it structured?**

The deck has 51 slides. Its contents page on slide 3 lists five sections, with The Home Category starting on slide 4, Competitive Advantages on slide 8, Growth Initiatives on slide 32, Financials on slide 41 and the Appendix after that.

**What does the Wayfair investor presentation say about Wayfair Verified performance?**

Slide 25 says Wayfair Verified items have >15x visits per SKU and >20x revenue per SKU, based on Q4 2024 data. Slide 24 describes Wayfair Verified as how Wayfair signals premium items that have been audited by its merchant team.

**What are the terms of the Wayfair REWARDS program in the Wayfair investor presentation?**

Slide 26 lists a $29 annual membership fee and 5% back in rewards on every item every day, with rewards that never expire. The same slide also lists member-exclusive sales, free shipping with restrictions, special offers and a member support line.

**Text**

Shopping for the Home Is Different
Home shopping is browse-based, / not search-based
Customers value individuality and have / an emotional connection to their homes
The Home category is largely / unbranded and very fragmented
Bulky and damage-prone products / require tailored delivery and / customer service

**Layout**

Full-width purple header bar with left-aligned title and a corner logo at right. Below, a 2x2 grid of outlined boxes on a white panel framed by pale lavender side strips and a horizontal lavender divider; each box has a row of three purple icons above centered text. Page number sits at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Purple flat-style icons in each card. Top-left: a laptop, a browser window with a cursor arrow, and a smartphone. Top-right: a house, a group of three people (silhouettes), and another house. Bottom-left: a potted plant, an armchair with a framed picture and a floor lamp, and a stand mixer. Bottom-right: a cardboard box with a logo, a hand truck carrying a box, and a shopping bag with logo. Top-right corner has a small logo made of grey and white rounded fragments forming a square ring. Purple header band across the top with the title at left and a logo at the top right. **Visuals**

Centre: a purple circular cycle diagram made of four thick curved arrows going clockwise (top arrowhead pointing right at the top, right arrowhead pointing down at the right, bottom arrowhead pointing left at the bottom, left arrowhead pointing up at the left). Inside the ring is a purple four-petal Wayfair-style logo mark above the text 'Technology and Infrastructure'. Four purple labels sit around the ring: Supplier Services (top left), Customer Service (top right), Shopping Experience (bottom right), Fulfillment (bottom left). Left panel: a purple globe icon (Americas visible) in a circle. Right panel: a purple icon of three people silhouettes in lighter and darker purple. Top-right corner: a small dark grey four-petal logo mark on the purple header.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with purple #7B189F header band and lavender #FAF2FF slanted side panels. White slide with a solid purple header bar (#7B189F) and pale lavender (#FAF2FF) slanted side panels. Title in white bold geometric sans-serif (Poppins-like). Headline in dark charcoal #363038, regular first line and bold second line. Diagram labels in bold purple #7B189F. Body text in regular Poppins-like sans-serif, charcoal #363038. ### Slide 9

**Text**

We have enormous reach across our >100M customer file
We have a household brand across / the US, Canada and the UK, with / broad customer awareness
We drove ~3B visits across site/app / in 2024
wayfair
Every style. Every home.
Fast & Free Shipping*
on orders over $35.
*Exclusions apply. Visit wayfair.com/shipping for details.
9

**Layout**

Full-width purple title bar across the top. Below it, the left half holds two black-outlined white text boxes stacked above a full-bleed photo of a purple ice cream truck. The right half is a pale lavender panel with a collage of rounded white-bordered brand images in a grid. A small page number 9 sits at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Left bottom: a photograph of a purple and white Wayfair-branded ice-cream-style truck parked on a residential street. A smiling woman in a white uniform and purple bow tie waves from the serving window, which has a striped awning and shelves of colourful stools and chairs on either side. A woman in a purple dress with a baby carrier stands in front of the truck beside a stroller. Daylight, trees and a house in the background, saturated purples and greens. Right collage, in rounded white-bordered tiles: a kraft paper Wayfair shopping bag; a smiling delivery man in a purple polo and cap holding a purple Wayfair box; a purple rounded banner with white Wayfair logo and tagline 'Every style. Every home.'; a rendering of a purple Wayfair warehouse with a car park; business cards with Wayfair logo on white and purple; the rear of a purple Wayfair truck; a purple Wayfair truck with a group of workers in hi-vis vests standing in front; three smartphones showing Wayfair app screens with a small app icon and a notification banner; a Fast & Free Shipping ad on lavender with a smiling woman holding stacked purple Wayfair boxes; three Wayfair catalogue covers showing room scenes. A Wayfair logo icon in dark purple sits at the top right of the title bar.

**Design**

Background: White (#FFFFFF) left, with lavender #F4E9FF panel on the right half and a purple #7B189F header bar. White background on the left, purple #7B189F title bar, lavender #F4E9FF right panel. Title text white bold rounded geometric sans-serif (Poppins/Gilroy style) at about 4% of slide height. Box text dark charcoal #363038 bold, about 4.2%, centred. Black 4px square-cornered outlines on text boxes. Collage tiles have white borders with rounded corners and soft shading.

### Slide 10

**Text**

our brand purpose
To Be the Trusted
Destination for All
Things Home
10

**Layout**

A tall purple panel fills the central half of the slide (x 25% to 75%, full height). A small pale label box sits at its top centre and a three-line centred white headline sits in the lower-middle of the panel. Photo tiles in a grid fill the left and right quarters. A small page number sits at the bottom right over the photos.

**Visuals**

Two mosaic grids of square-ish product and room-interior photo tiles with thin white gutters, one on the left quarter and one on the right quarter, partly hidden by the purple panel. Tiles show a beige toaster, a dining room with plants, a pixelated blurred tile, a green metal bed with pillows, a black-and-white abstract pillow, a pink arched hallway, a table lamp with a patterned base, a yellow pegboard with bikes, a pink room with a wooden stool and green bag, a mint record player, a pink bedroom, stacked blue and striped linens, a green dining set, a gold-framed mirror, an outdoor patio with rattan stools, a vase with red flowers, a leopard print above a shelf, a spindle bed with blue gingham bedding, a pink dining room with a round table, a bathroom with a pedestal sink, a pantry shelving unit, a round pouf, a washer and dryer, blue and yellow pillows, a pool with rattan chairs, a desk with a chair, a blue knit throw, a plant in a living room, a red side table, a kids' room with a green chair, a yellow attic room with a pink couch, a woven basket with a plant, a pink fridge, a kids' shelf in a light blue room, a navy recliner, a patterned throw, a colourful bookshelf with a navy sofa, a small round side table, a navy chair with a throw, a nursery with a dresser, a white fireplace with blue chairs, a Lego head on a cube shelf, an orange vase and a blue tiled kitchen. A purple rectangle sits over the centre and a pale lavender rectangle holds the label.

**Design**

Background: Mosaic of product and room photo tiles with white gutters, covered in the middle by a solid purple #7B189F panel. Bold, playful, brand-led look. Solid purple #7B189F centre panel, white headline, pale lavender #FAF2FF label box with purple text. Geometric rounded sans-serif (Poppins) in SemiBold/Bold weight. Headline about 7.9% of slide height, label about 2.7%. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

11

**Layout**

Full-width purple title bar across the top with left-aligned white title and the Wayfair icon at the far right. A three-tier pyramid sits in the center, with Perigold and Wayfair brand blocks on the left and AllModern, Birch Lane and Joss & Main blocks on the right, joined to the pyramid by purple connector lines. Slide number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Pyramid of three tiers: a pale lavender triangle top (Luxury), a purple trapezoid middle (Mass) and a dark charcoal trapezoid base (Economy), with white gaps between tiers. Thin purple elbow connector lines run from Perigold and Wayfair to the left of the pyramid and from AllModern/Birch Lane to the right. Perigold connects to the Luxury tier, Wayfair connects to the Mass and Economy tiers via a vertical line at the left, and a bracket on the right links Luxury and Mass to AllModern and Birch Lane. Logos: Wayfair white four-diamond icon in top-right bar; Perigold black spaced serif wordmark; purple Wayfair wordmark with icon; ALLMODERN wordmark (thin ALL, bold MODERN); BIRCH LN serif wordmark; JOSS & MAIN thin serif wordmark. A 2x2 grid of circular photos at lower left: a blue armchair in a light room (Kelly Clarkson Home), terracotta bedding on a bed (Re/Fine), a black sink with a gold faucet (Kohler), and a yellow Dutch oven with food (Staub). Below each photo is its logo: KELLY CLARKSON home script, RE/FINE, THE BOLD LOOK OF KOHLER, and a red and black staub logo.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a #7B189F purple bar across the top 7.2%. White background with a purple #7B189F header bar. Title is white bold geometric sans (Poppins-like). Pyramid tiers are #FAF2FF, #7719A1 and #363038, with dark text on the light tier and white text on the others. Body captions are #414141 Poppins-like regular, with the tagline in bold italic. Purple connector lines are about 1.5px. Flat style with no shadows or gradients. Title is about 4% of slide height; captions about 1.7%.

### Slide 12

**Text**

We can tell a brand story everywhere, meeting the customer where they are
Physical Retail
Influencers & Social Media
Homepage Placement
Throughout the Funnel
Catalogs, Circulars & Mailers
Prominent Features on Category Pages
12

**Layout**

Purple title bar across the top with left-aligned white title and a small logo at the top right. Below, a purple field holds six pale lavender panels: left column has two stacked panels (Physical Retail, Throughout the Funnel), centre column has a tall panel (Influencers & Social Media) above a shorter wide panel (Prominent Features on Category Pages), right column has two stacked panels (Homepage Placement, Catalogs, Circulars & Mailers). Each panel has a centred bold heading at the top and screenshots below. Page number sits at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Physical Retail: rendering of a Wayfair store, a purple modern building with a white wayfair logo, parking lot full of cars in front, blue sky with clouds, purple border. Influencers & Social Media: three smartphone mockups side by side. Left: Instagram Reel of a blonde woman in a purple top pushing a stroller on a purple bench with the caption 'Ranking Nursery Must-Haves' and gold, silver and bronze medal icons. Middle: 'Explore the Collection by KOHLER x STUDIO MCGEE' with a blue 'TAP TO SHOP MORE' link and a photo of a blonde woman in a black top and white trousers in a kitchen. Right: Wayfair Posts screen showing a woman in a purple suit holding a Pomeranian dog, with the wayfair logo and 'Every style. Every home.' and 'Perfection.' Homepage Placement: screenshot of the Wayfair homepage with a nav bar, a patio hero image with a Sunbrella card ('OUR TOP BRANDS', 'Sink into comfort that really performs.', 'Shop Now' button), and tiles on a mint background: 'Up to 50% OFF Indoor & Outdoor Rugs', 'Up to 40% OFF Hot Tubs & Pools', 'Up to 40% OFF Grills & More', 'Up to 40% OFF Fire Pits & Patio Heaters', 'Up to 50% OFF Outdoor Dining'. Throughout the Funnel: a purple 'DOORBUSTER DEAL' banner with '$100 OFF NOW $199 Blackstone 2-Burner Gas Grill' and a yellow 'Shop Now' button, with a griddle grill image; below it a cream banner 'Top Brands. BIG Deals. Up to 20% OFF spring cleaning go-tos from BISSELL and more →' with a vacuum cleaner image. Prominent Features on Category Pages: a 'Shop by Brand' strip of seven brand tiles (nora, Tempur-Pedic, sleep, Casper, Sealy, Beautyrest, Serta) each with a mattress/bedroom photo and a circular arrow at the right. Catalogs, Circulars & Mailers: two mailer pages, one lavender 'Can't-miss kitchen scores' with kitchenware product grid, one purple/white 'Hardworking appliances from $69 PER MONTH' with a dishwasher, range and refrigerator and a purple wayfair footer. A small Wayfair icon logo in white and grey sits at top right of the title bar.

**Design**

Background: Solid purple #7B189F with a white strip between title bar and body. Rich purple #7B189F background with a thin white line below the title bar. Title white bold geometric sans-serif (Montserrat-style SemiBold) about 4% of slide height. Panels pale lavender #FAF2FF with dark purple-black #2B1F3F bold headings about 3.2% of height. Screenshots have thin purple borders and light drop shadows. No rounded corners on panels.

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: slide 14 is an overlay that hides the value-chain diagram beneath it, some pages are sparse or low in information (19, 29 to 31, 39), and the two revenue and gross margin charts (42, 43) use small labels and dual series that are hard to read.
- Suggested repair: Fix the overlap on slide 14, give the gross margin bar on slide 47 a label and a value, and enlarge the data labels and legends on slides 42 to 45.

**Layout**

Full-width purple header band with left-aligned title and a logo at the right corner. Below it, a value-chain diagram of white boxes and chevrons is largely covered by a large centered rounded purple panel (about 71% wide) with a dark header row and three numbered rows; page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

A value-chain diagram sits behind the panel. Top row: four white boxes/chevrons with thin purple outlines labelled Inbound, Fulfillment, Delivery, Returns & Incidents. The last one is a pentagon-like chevron with a slanted right edge. Icons: a container ship with crane and stacked containers at the left (Inbound), partly hidden by the panel; the other icons are hidden. Bottom row: a wide white band with a purple outline, a slanted right end, and a thin horizontal line near the right. It holds a line-drawn head-and-shoulders person icon (Customer Service) at the left, with labels Supplier Service and Customer Service mostly hidden by the panel. On top of this, a rounded-corner panel (large radius at the top corners, rounded bottom corners) with a dark plum header row 'All our services enable...' and three medium-purple rows separated by thin white lines. Top-right of the header: white Wayfair logo, a four-piece rounded cross/diamond mark.

**Design**

Background: Light blush #F6F0F0 with a purple #7B189F band across the top 7.2%. Header band purple #7B189F with white bold text. Slide background light blush #F6F0F0. Title about 3.7% of slide height, panel header about 5.9%, row text about 4.9%, diagram labels about 4% in purple #7B189F. Thin purple outlines on white shapes. Page number dark grey #363038, small. No shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Full-width purple header band holds the left-aligned title and a corner logo at the right. A bold subtitle sits below at the left margin. Two white column cards (Dropship, CastleGate) sit on the right with row labels in the middle and purple category blocks bleeding off the left edge. Horizontal purple rules separate the rows. The page number sits at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Comparison table with two white rounded-top cards, Dropship (left) and CastleGate (right). Rows: Speed – Order-to-delivery time, in calendar days: Dropship 5.4, CastleGate 2.9; Badging share, in %: Dropship 47%, CastleGate 88%. Cost – Return rate, in %: Dropship 4.8%, CastleGate 3.8%; Incident rate, in %: Dropship 3.8%, CastleGate 3.4%. A green circle (about #66A94F) follows every CastleGate value (four in total). Two purple rounded-right-corner blocks labelled Speed and Cost sit at the left edge. Thin purple horizontal lines at the card header bottom, between Speed and Cost, and under the last row. A white Wayfair-style rounded-diamond logo sits at the top right in the header band.

**Design**

Background: #F6F0F0 pale pink-grey with a #7B189F purple header band across the top 7.2%. Pale pinkish-grey background #F6F0F0 with a purple #7B189F header band. White title text, bold geometric sans-serif (Poppins), about 4% of slide height. Subtitle is dark purple-grey #3F2A5A, bold plus regular weight. Row labels are regular weight in dark purple #51006A at about 3.5%. Values and column headers are bold purple #7B189F. Category blocks are purple with white bold text and rounded right corners of about 3% radius. Cards are white with small rounded top corners. No shadows or gradients. Lines are 1px purple. Green dots are flat.

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the section structure, the purple title bar system, the badge-lift flow on slide 16, and the three-step roadmap on slide 46.

Speed gets rewarded on our website
Sales significantly increase
● Different speed badges for / 1, 2, 3, 5 days
● More exposure on site

No badge
>20%

**Layout**

Full-width purple title bar at top with left-aligned white title and a logo at the top right. Below, two columns with bold headings and purple arrow underlines: left column has bullets above a website screenshot, right column has bullets above a vertical badge flow diagram. Footnote under the right column, page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Top-right corner: a small purple/dark rounded four-piece Wayfair-style logo mark on the purple bar. Left: a screenshot of a Wayfair search results page for 'end table' with a left filter sidebar (Fast Delivery toggle switched on, Size, Width, Height, Overall Depth, Color/Finish) and a heading 'End & Side Tables (Fast Delivery) 6,040 Items' with a 'Fast Delivery' filter chip. Two product cards: a black round end table with drawer and a wooden side table with shelf. Red rectangles highlight the Fast Delivery toggle, the Fast Delivery chip and the 'FREE 3-Day Delivery' badge. Diagram on right: a vertical list of badge stages (No badge, 5-day badge, 3-day badge, 1-day badge) connected by thin green downward arrows, with green percentage labels (>20%, >10%, >10%) between them. A teal bracket line runs from beside 'No badge' right and down, then back left with an arrowhead pointing to '1-day badge', with a teal '>60%' label at its middle. Under each column heading is a thick purple line; the left one ends in a large purple arrowhead pointing right.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a purple #7B189F band across the top 7.2%. White background with a solid purple #7B189F title bar. Title in white bold geometric sans-serif (Poppins-style) about 4% of slide height. Headings and body text in dark charcoal #363038 Poppins, headings semibold/bold ~3%, body regular ~3%. Accent purple #7B189F for lines and arrow; green #93C47D and #6AA84F for percentages and arrows; teal #01768B for the bracket and >60%. Flat shapes, no shadows or gradients.

A circular cycle diagram of three thick purple curved arrows (about #7B189F) pointing clockwise around the text 'More exposure and more sales'. A lavender-purple person bust silhouette icon beside point 3. A thin grey horizontal line under point 1, a thin vertical grey line left of point 2, and a thin grey horizontal line above point 3. A purple four-piece diamond/rosette logo in the top-right corner.

**Layout**

Full-width purple title banner at top with left-aligned title and a logo at the top right. Below, two sections: a left column with a vertical five-step process flow and a right panel with a pale grey-pink background holding a bar comparison and a table of numbers. A chevron sits between the sections. Footnote is centred at the bottom and the page number is at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Left: five icons stacked vertically, each beside a label: a purple shopping bag with a CastleGate logo (Order), an open purple cardboard box with logo (CastleGate), a purple hand truck with a package (Pick & Pack), a purple delivery truck (Ship), a purple check mark (Deliver). Pale lavender down arrows connect the steps. A thin dark vertical line separates the left column from the right panel, broken in the middle where a purple double chevron pointing right sits. Right: a bar graphic with two groups, Small Parcel and Large Parcel, each with a pale lilac bar (Dropship) and a taller solid purple bar (CastleGate) on a thin grey baseline, with no printed values. Small Parcel bars are about 11% and 15.5% of slide height; Large Parcel bars are about 8% and 15.5%. A dark diagonal arrow rises from above the Dropship bar to above the CastleGate bar in each group. A solid purple box labelled Conversion uplift is on the left of the bars. Below is a purple box 'Order to delivery days' followed by four white boxes with values ~5, ~3, ~15, ~7. Thin dark purple underlines sit under the two section headings and under Small Parcel and Large Parcel. A CastleGate logo, a dark rounded four-part mark, is at the top right of the banner.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a purple #7B189F header band across the top 7.2%. White background, a solid purple #7B189F header band, and a pale pink-grey #F6F0F0 panel on the right. Title text is white in a bold geometric sans-serif (Poppins SemiBold) at about 4% of slide height. Section headings are purple #7B189F in bold Poppins at about 3.7%. Step labels are dark charcoal #363038 in bold Poppins at 3.5%. Italic bold purple sub-labels are at 2.2%. The palette is purple #7B189F, lilac #F2DEFF, lavender arrows #E0DAF2 and dark charcoal. Shapes have square corners and no shadows or gradients.

19

**Layout**

Full-width purple header bar with left-aligned title and a small logo at the top right. Below it is a full-width light lilac band with a centered subtitle. The diagram runs across the lower area: a bold label on the left, a curly brace with five stacked items, then a plus sign, 'Wayfair Margin', an equals sign and 'Retail Price' in a row to the right. The page number sits at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

A tall thin black curly brace, opening to the left, spans the five cost items. Its stroke is about 2px. It runs from about x 21.5% to 24%, and from y 33% to 85%. Its point is at about x 21.5%, y 59%. A bold purple plus sign (#7B189F) sits at x 50.9%, y 54%, about 5.4% wide and 8.6% tall. A purple equals sign made of two horizontal bars sits at about x 75.5%, y 55% to 61%, about 5.1% wide. A small dark four-lobed pinwheel/cross logo sits in the top right corner of the purple header.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. The header bar is purple #7B189F with white bold text. The subtitle band is light lilac #F2DEFF with bold purple #7B189F text. Cost items are in regular-weight dark charcoal #332C36 text. The left, margin and price labels are bold, in #363038 and #332C36. Typeface is Poppins, a geometric sans-serif. The header title is about 4% of the slide height. The subtitle and items are about 4.4%, and the large bold labels about 4.9%. There are no shadows or gradients, and the shapes are flat with square corners.

### Slide 20

**Text**

At Wayfair, we have an extensive offering across everything Home
CORE CATEGORIES
Sofas & Sectionals
Rugs
Bedroom Furniture
Outdoor Decor
Youth Products
Casegoods
Kitchen & Dining
Office Furniture
Entertainment Furniture
Accent Furniture
Consumers broadly know us for our core categories - Sofas, Casegoods, Bedroom furniture and more
These are areas of the business where we have high awareness and high online market share
These are areas we have invested in from a resource and marketing perspective for many years
20

**Layout**

Full-width purple title bar across the top with left-aligned title and a small logo at the far right. Left 58% holds a pale lavender panel with a centered heading and a 4-column grid of category photo tiles (4 on row 1, 4 on row 2, 2 centered-left on row 3), each with a purple label band. Right side has three bulleted paragraphs left-aligned; page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Ten category tiles, each a product photo above a purple label band. Sofas & Sectionals: cream modular sofa with a round wood table in a bright room. Rugs: tufted brown leather sofa on a colourful patterned rug with a brass side table. Bedroom Furniture: wooden dresser and bed in a room with a dark grey wall. Outdoor Decor: blue-cushioned wicker patio sofa set around a fire table with greenery. Youth Products: natural wood crib against a pale wallpaper with a moon. Casegoods: white bookcase with colourful books and a yellow LEGO head. Kitchen & Dining: wooden round dining table with X-back chairs against a terracotta wall. Office Furniture: standing desk with monitor and laptop. Entertainment Furniture: light wood media console under a wall-mounted TV. Accent Furniture: round wood coffee table with flowers in front of a rattan cabinet. A small white Wayfair-style logo mark (four rounded diamond shapes) sits in the top right of the title bar. Three black round bullet dots precede the paragraphs.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF. White slide background with a purple #7B189F title bar and a lavender #FAF2FF panel. Title in white bold geometric sans-serif (Poppins SemiBold), about 4% of slide height. Panel heading in black #000000 bold Poppins caps, about 4.2%. Body text in dark charcoal #363038 Poppins, about 3.2%, mixing SemiBold and Light weights for emphasis. Label bands are purple with white semibold Poppins about 1.7%. No shadows, no rounded corners, no gradients.

### Slide 21

**Text**

We also have strong and growing businesses in several emerging categories
EMERGING CATEGORIES
Large Appliances
Outdoor structures & Spa
Cabinetry
Plumbing
Small Electrics
Storage & Org
Kitchen and Tabletop
Recreation
Pet
Bedding
Decor
Wall Accents
We are focused on driving share gains in frequency purchases across these groups
21

**Layout**

Full-width purple header band with left-aligned white title and a logo at the top right. Below, a pale lavender panel holds a centered heading and a 4-column by 3-row grid of product photos, each with a purple label bar beneath. The lower two rows are enclosed by a dark rounded-corner-free outline box. A single-line takeaway sits under the grid, and the page number is at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Twelve product photos in a 4x3 grid, each with a purple caption bar: Row 1: Large Appliances (a stainless French-door refrigerator in a white kitchen with wood cabinetry and red produce), Outdoor structures & Spa (a wooden gazebo with a dark roof over outdoor sofas, trees and blue sky behind), Cabinetry (grey-beige kitchen cabinets with glass-front uppers and a window), Plumbing (a bathroom with mirrors, a black vanity, a freestanding tub and a black faucet, stone tile). Row 2, inside the outline: Small Electrics (a dark grey Ninja air fryer next to a stove and a plate of food), Storage & Org (white fabric storage bins on shelves), Kitchen and Tabletop (stacks of white plates and bowls, a fork, a lime and a plant), Recreation (a pool table in a grey room). Row 3, inside the outline: Pet (a curly brown dog lying on a cream pet bed in a living room), Bedding (stacked white pillows and folded white bedding), Decor (dark brown ceramic vases on a floor by a white wall), Wall Accents (two decorative fish wall sculptures, one dark blue and one white). A dark charcoal rectangular outline about 6 px thick surrounds rows 2 and 3. A small dark four-lobed logo sits at the top right of the header.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a lavender #FAF2FF panel and a purple #7B189F header band. White slide with a purple header band #7B189F and a pale lavender content panel #FAF2FF. Title in white bold geometric sans-serif (Poppins SemiBold) at about 4% of slide height. Heading in black bold Poppins at about 4.2%, uppercase. Labels are white semi-bold Poppins at about 1.7%. Footer sentence is black Poppins light with bold on the phrase 'share gains in frequency purchases', about 3.7%. Outline charcoal #363038. No shadows or gradients, square corners.

Emerging category growth rates are based on gross revenue, prior to returns and cancellations. U.S. net revenue represents reported segment revenue.

**Layout**

Full-width purple title band across the top with the title left-aligned and a logo at the far right. Below it a large rectangular outlined chart box fills most of the slide, with the chart title top-left inside it, category labels right-aligned at the left of the bars, and the axis title at the bottom centre. Page number at bottom right and a footnote at bottom left beneath the box.

**Visuals**

Horizontal bar chart in a dark charcoal-bordered box (about 3px stroke). Title: 'Emerging Category Growth (CAGR 2018 - 2024)' on a pale lavender label. X axis 'CAGR %' from 0% to 30% with ticks at 0%, 10%, 20%, 30% and light grey vertical gridlines; thin dark vertical axis line at 0%. Ten solid purple (#7B189F) bars, sorted descending, no printed values, estimated: Outdoor structures & Spa about 26%, Hardware about 22.7%, Recreation about 18%, Appliances about 17.2%, Pet about 16.7%, Flooring about 14.1%, Vanities about 13.4%, Storage & Org about 12.9%, Core Plumbing about 12.9%, U.S. Net Revenue about 10.1%. No legend. A dark charcoal logo made of four rounded diamond/petal shapes forming an X-like emblem sits at the top right in the title band.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a full-width purple #7B189F band across the top 7.2%. White background with a purple #7B189F title band. Title in white bold geometric sans-serif (Poppins SemiBold) about 3.6% of slide height. Chart title, category labels and axis title in purple #7B189F bold Poppins; chart title about 2.9%, labels about 2.2%. Axis tick labels in grey Arial-like sans-serif. Pale lavender #FAF2FF label backgrounds. Page number and footnote in dark #363038 Poppins; footnote very small, about 1.2%. Flat style, no shadows or gradients, bars have slight rounded corners.

### Slide 23

**Text**

Curation is our differentiator, helping consumers cut through the noise
Instill Confidence
Provide / inspiration
Celebrate / the season
Reinforce / trust
A shortcut / to the best items
Cohesive looks / and ideas to / enhance the room
Trend-forward, / seasonally relevant / storytelling
Collaborations / with influencers / our customers / know and love
23

**Layout**

Full-width purple title bar across the top with left-aligned white title and a small logo at the right end. Below it, four equal columns, each with a visual at the top, a purple header block, and a pale lilac body card with centred text. Page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Four column visuals plus a logo. Column 1: a round purple 'WAYFAIR VERIFIED' badge with white curved text and a white scalloped seal holding a purple checkmark. Column 2: a photo of a cosy living room with a curved cream bouclé sofa, navy, rust and red pillows, a round wooden coffee table with glasses, a decorated Christmas tree at left, a fireplace with a garland at right and bookshelves behind, in warm light. A small white 'JOSS & MAIN' wordmark tag sits at its top-left corner. Column 3: a Wayfair campaign image with a green grass background, a large white 'wayfair' wordmark with its logo mark, and an overhead view of a wooden table set with colourful plates, bread, fruit and glasses, flanked by red and pink chairs, with a small round lilac 'LATE SPRING 2024' badge at the bottom right. Column 4: a photo of a smiling woman in a patterned tweed jacket standing by a dining table set with red flowers, candles, glasses and teal-and-white plates, with a white fireplace and pendant lamps behind and a wreath at the right. Each photo has a subtle drop shadow. The top right of the title bar holds a white-and-dark rounded-diamond Wayfair logo mark. Card blocks have soft drop shadows.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White slide with a purple title bar #7B189F. White bold geometric sans-serif titles (Poppins SemiBold). Header blocks are purple #7B189C. Body cards are pale lilac #FAF2FF with dark grey #363038 Poppins regular text. The word 'best' is italic. Title size about 4% of slide height, header text about 4%, body text about 3.5%. Cards are square-cornered with soft shadows.

…leading to much higher revenue
>15x
visits
per SKU
>20x
revenue1

**Layout**

Full-width purple title banner across the top with left-aligned white title and a small logo at the top right. Two equal columns below, each with a purple bold heading, then a boxed bar graphic with a large multiplier and label at left. A centered two-line footnote sits at the bottom.

**Visuals**

Two boxed bar-comparison graphics, each with a thin pale-lavender border and a soft drop shadow on a white card. Left card: a short pale-lavender bar (about 5% of the tall bar's height) at lower left next to a tall solid purple bar (#881294) at right, and a black arrow rising from above the short bar toward the top-left of the tall bar. Right card: the same composition with a shorter pale-lavender bar, a tall purple bar, and a black diagonal arrow. No axes, gridlines or values are printed. Small logo icon at top right of the banner: a dark grey/black four-piece rounded diamond cluster.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a purple #7B189F banner across the top 7.2%. White background with a solid purple banner (#7B189F) at the top, about 7% of the slide height. Title in white bold geometric sans-serif (Poppins SemiBold), about 4% of the slide height. Column headings in bold purple #670080, Poppins, about 4.2% of the height. Big numbers in black Poppins bold at about 7.9%. Labels in black Poppins regular, about 4.9% for 'visits' and 'revenue', 2.5% for 'per SKU'. Footnote in grey #595959 at about 1.5%. The cards have a faint lavender outline and a subtle shadow with square corners. Bars are flat fills with no gradient.

Right: a laughing woman with long brown hair in a lilac and orange checked cardigan, holding up a gold faceted lamp base with a white drum shade, against a mint green backdrop, framed from the waist up with bright studio light.

### Slide 30

**Text**

We are reshaping many of our current capabilities
with Generative AI.
Marketing
Search, Recs &
Personalization
Merchandising
Pricing
Supply Chain
Sales & Service
Over a decade of ML and AI
Gen AI
30

**Layout**

Centered two-line title in the upper third. Below it a single row of six equally spaced circles with a label under each, left to right. A legend sits at the bottom left and the page number at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Six circular icon badges in a row. Each circle is split horizontally: top half light lavender #F2DEFF (Gen AI), bottom half pale pink-beige #F0E4E4 (over a decade of ML and AI). The Pricing circle is entirely beige. Each holds a purple flat illustration. 1) Marketing: a megaphone pointing right with three small sound lines. 2) Search, Recs & Personalization: a magnifying glass with a white lens and purple handle angled to the lower right. 3) Merchandising: a purple armchair with a framed picture on the left and a floor lamp on the right. 4) Pricing: a purple price tag with a string and two horizontal lines. 5) Supply Chain: a purple delivery truck facing right with two dark wheels. 6) Sales & Service: two overlapping purple speech bubbles. The legend at bottom left has a beige dot labelled 'Over a decade of ML and AI' and a lavender dot labelled 'Gen AI'. 'Generative AI' in the title has a lavender highlight rectangle behind it.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in dark charcoal #363038, bold geometric sans-serif (Poppins Bold), about 6% of slide height. Labels in Poppins SemiBold, about 2.7% of slide height, #363038. Legend and page number in Poppins Regular/Medium, small. Accents are lavender #F2DEFF, beige #F0E4E4 and purples #A878DC and #5E2A7E in the icons. Flat shapes, no shadows or gradients.

31

**Layout**

Centered two-line title at top with 'new Gen AI' on a lavender highlight bar. Below, a grid of circular icons: a row of six (y about 24-45%) and a row of five (y about 58-79%, left-aligned, last slot empty), each with a centered bold label beneath. A two-item colour legend sits at bottom left and the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Eleven circular badges, each about 11.6% wide, holding flat purple icons. The top-row circles for Search, Marketing, Merchandising, Supply Chain and Sales & Service are split: lavender (#F2DEFF) top half and blush-grey (#F0E4E4) bottom half. Pricing is fully blush-grey. The bottom-row circles are fully lavender. Icons: 1) magnifying glass with pixelated lens and handle; 2) pixelated megaphone with sound lines; 3) armchair with framed picture and floor lamp; 4) hanging price tag with lines; 5) delivery truck; 6) two overlapping speech bubbles; 7) side table with shelf (Muse); 8) smartphone with globe; 9) browser window with cursor; 10) piggy bank with coin and sparks; 11) shield with check mark. Legend: a blush-grey circle labelled 'Over a decade of ML and AI' and a lavender circle labelled 'Gen AI'.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in dark charcoal #363038, heavy geometric sans-serif (Poppins SemiBold/Bold), about 5.9% of slide height, with a lavender #F2DEFF highlight rectangle behind 'new Gen AI'. Labels are Poppins SemiBold about 2.7% in #363038. Legend and page number are Poppins regular, about 2.2% and 2%. Icons use medium purple #9B6FD6 and dark purple #5B2B8C. Flat shapes, no shadows or gradients.

Full-width purple title bar across the top with the title left-aligned and a small logo at the top-right corner. Centered Wayfair Professional logo and tagline below, then a full-width pale lilac band with two centered bullets. Lower half is a photo grid: two large photos side by side, then a row of five equal photos beneath, each with a dark translucent caption strip at its bottom. Page number sits at the bottom right over the last photo.

**Visuals**

Wayfair Professional logo: purple four-petal diamond/knot mark, then lowercase 'wayfair' in purple with a small registered mark, then 'PROFESSIONAL' in dark purple bold capitals. Small white-on-purple Wayfair knot icon in top-right of title bar (cropped to the corner, appears dark purple/black-ish). Seven photographs. Interior Designers: living room with a cream sectional sofa with black-and-white geometric and orange pillows, green knit throw, walnut side table, round wood coffee table with books and a dark bowl, black-and-white striped rug, grey bouclé swivel chair, light wood floor. Office: overhead view of an office lounge with wood parquet floor, wooden table with white chairs and notebooks, dark grey velvet armchairs, an arc floor lamp, an organic-shaped wood coffee table with magazines, grey carpet, and a beige sofa. Contractor: bright bathroom with a double black-faucet vanity, mirrors, freestanding white tub, large window with green trees. Accommodations: hotel bedroom with a white upholstered bed with a navy runner and pillows, bench, wood nightstand with lamp, teal wall and tall windows. Food Service: restaurant with wooden tables with gold legs, cream chairs, grey tufted banquette, place settings, pendant lamps. Education: classroom with long light wood tables, red plastic chairs, a pegboard with tools, a wall clock, big windows to autumn trees. Property Management: amenity room with a pool table with X-shaped legs, orange chairs at a dining table, kitchen shelves, windows to a city view, blue geometric rug. Each photo has a black overlay strip at its bottom at 44% opacity holding white caption text.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF. White background; purple #7B189F title bar with white bold text; lilac #FAF2FF band with purple bold bullets; tagline in dark grey #363038. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif (Poppins/Gilroy-like), semi-bold to bold. Title about 4% of slide height, bullets about 4%, tagline about 3.2%, large captions 6.7–8.2%, small captions 2.7%. Square corners, no shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

**Visuals**

Four light-grey (#EEEEEE) square cards, each with a big serif number and a small sans-serif label. A white serif 'P' monogram logo sits at the left of the dark band. The bottom is a full-width photograph, top-down, of a person with dark short hair and a dark jacket seen from above. Both hands with gold and silver rings and a tan-strap watch rest on a dark grey denim-like fabric with white beaded stitching. Beside them are a black-and-white geometric ceramic tile, three grey elongated glossy tiles, and cream textured fabric swatches with arc patterns at the upper right and a grey woven swatch at the lower right. Moody, dark navy-charcoal tones with soft light. A white PERIGOLD wordmark sits over the photo at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF at the top, with a charcoal #212121 band in the middle and a full-bleed photograph at the bottom. White top background, charcoal #212121 band, light-grey #EEEEEE cards. Stat numbers are black serif at about 7.4% of slide height. Labels are black Montserrat-style geometric sans-serif at about 3% of slide height. Flat shapes, square corners, no shadows or gradients. Wordmark is white serif capitals with wide letter-spacing.

**Layout**

Two text columns on the right: a narrower bulleted column starting at about 34% and a second column at about 68%, each with a serif heading and bullets. **Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of a light grey textured surface with two Perigold printed catalogues. At upper left a folded brochure stands at an angle, tilted about 30 degrees; its cover shows a bright staircase interior with a green plant, a black horse sculpture and a wooden curved table, with the black serif word PERIGOLD, small text 'THE DESTINATION FOR LUXURY HOME FURNISHINGS', 'PERIGOLD.COM' and a '10% OFF' offer tag. At the bottom centre an open catalogue spread is rotated, showing a terrace with lanterns and a mountain view, a bedroom or sitting-room photo on its left page, and partial text 'UNCOMPROMISING QUALITY' and 'UNPRE...'. Soft daylight, soft shadows, mostly white, grey, green and tan colours. A small white PERIGOLD wordmark logo sits at bottom-left.

Body bullets are black in Montserrat-like geometric sans-serif regular. Title is about 5.9% of slide height, headings about 4.4%, body about 3%. Bullets are solid black circles. Page number is grey (#595959). No shadows or gradients on text.

**Layout**

Full-width purple title banner across the top with left-aligned title and a corner logo at the right. Below, three equal columns each with a brand banner, a photo directly beneath it and a centered four-line customer profile; page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Three brand banners with wordmarks, each above a room photograph. Left: ALLMODERN banner in royal blue with white wordmark (ALL thin, MODERN bold, wide-spaced) and tagline 'Modern made simple.'; photo of a bright modern living room with a cream sofa with a blue cushion, a light wood coffee table, a teal rug, a wooden sideboard, colourful framed art on the wall and tall cacti seen through glass doors. Middle: BIRCH LN banner in dark olive green with white serif wordmark and italic-accent tagline; photo of a sunny breakfast nook with a round wood pedestal table, four rush-seat wooden chairs, a white pleated pendant lamp, French doors to a garden, sage green cabinets and a round white rug on stone tile. Right: JOSS & MAIN banner in off-white with black thin sans-serif wordmark and serif small-caps tagline; photo of an outdoor terrace lounge with black and white striped sectional sofas, orange and white pillows, a round white coffee table, a woven rug and a hazy hillside view at a peach-pink sunset sky. Small dark corner logo (four-lobed shape) in the top right of the purple banner. Photos are rectangular, uncropped boxes, no shadows.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a purple #7B189F band across the top 7.2%. White background with a saturated purple (#7B189F) title bar. Title in white bold rounded geometric sans-serif (Quicksand-like bold) about 3.8% of slide height. Body text in dark charcoal (#363038) Montserrat-like/Nunito-style sans-serif at about 2.7% height, with bold labels and regular values. Brand banners: blue #1A199E, green #3C4A26, off-white #F4F0EC. No rounded corners, no gradients.

### Slide 37

**Text**

We’ve focused our international efforts across Canada, the UK and Ireland
Logistics
Optimized supply chain for the best sellers, to ship fast with reduced incidences:
CastleGate warehouses located across regions
CA + UK & IE
Dedicated teams by geography
Dedicated teams to serve Canadian and UK customers:
Localized support available for customers and throughout sales funnel
Marketing and customer acquisition
Extended reach for existing and new customers:
Over 500 million visits across Wayfair CA, UK and IE over 2024
Canada, the UK and Ireland represent ~$50-100B of addressable market opportunity
Source: Wayfair Estimates, Euromonitor, UK ONS, Canada Statistics Bureau
37

**Layout**

Full-width purple title bar across the top with left-aligned title. Three overlapping circles in a triangle arrangement in the center (Logistics top, Dedicated teams bottom-left, Marketing bottom-right) with a small cream circle at the overlap. Callout text blocks sit left and right of the circles, each linked by a thin line with dot ends. A pale lavender band with a purple statement spans the bottom; source footnote bottom-left and page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Three large overlapping circles: top circle in purple #7B189F labelled Logistics; bottom-left circle in deep plum #51006A labelled Dedicated teams by geography; bottom-right circle in pale lilac #F2DEFF labelled Marketing and customer acquisition. Each has a faint light outline. A small cream circle #F6F0F0 at the center overlap reads CA + UK & IE. Three thin dark lines with dot ends connect circles to the callout texts: from the Logistics circle up-right to the supply chain text, from the plum circle up-left to the left text, from the lilac circle right to the right text. A small dark four-petal/diamond cluster logo sits at the top-right of the title bar. A small logo image sits bottom-left over a white patch near the source line.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF. White background, purple #7B189F title bar with white bold text. Rounded geometric sans-serif (Poppins) in semibold/bold for headings and regular for descriptions. Dark gray #363038 callout text; circle labels white on dark circles and #3F2A5A on the lilac circle. Bottom band #FAF2FF with purple bold statement. Title about 4% of slide height, circle labels about 4.2%, callout headers about 3.2%, callout body about 2.5%. No shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Full-width purple title bar across the top with left-aligned white title and a small dark logo at the far right. A large US map occupies the left ~63% of the slide below the bar; on the right a column of brand logos each followed by starred store lines, left-aligned at about 65% across. Thin grey leader lines run from the circled states (MA, IL, TX, FL) to the logos.

**Visuals**

Outline map of the US (all 50 states, black outlines on white, state abbreviation labels, Alaska and Hawaii inset at lower left). Four purple ring circles highlight MA (northeast), IL (Illinois), TX (Texas) and FL (Florida). Thin dark grey leader lines connect IL to the second JOSS & MAIN logo and to the wayfair logo, TX to the second ALLMODERN logo, and FL to the BIRCH LN logo. Logos: ALLMODERN wordmark (light 'ALL' plus bold 'MODERN', black, spaced caps), JOSS & MAIN thin serif wordmark in dark grey, wayfair logo with purple four-diamond icon and purple lowercase 'wayfair' wordmark, BIRCH LN dark olive-green serif wordmark with small-caps 'LN'. A small dark charcoal Wayfair-style diamond cluster icon sits in the top right of the purple bar. Black star bullets precede each store line.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a purple #7B189F band across the top 7.2%. White background with a solid purple #7B189F header bar. Title in white bold geometric sans-serif (Poppins-like), about 4% of slide height. Body text in black Poppins-style medium sans-serif, about 2.2% of slide height. Accents purple #7B189F circles with about 6px stroke, no fill. No shadows or gradients.

**Visuals**

Clustered bar chart with 10 year categories 2015-2024, no axes lines or gridlines. Each year has two adjacent bars: light steel-blue (#5B93B2) Orders (M) on the left and dark navy-teal (#024765) Net Revenue ($B) on the right. Orders values printed above the light bars in steel blue: 2015 9, 2016 14, 2017 19, 2018 28, 2019 38, 2020 61, 2021 52, 2022 40, 2023 41, 2024 40. Net revenue values printed in white at the base of the dark bars: $2, $3, $5, $7, $9, $14, $14, $12, $12, $12. Dark bar heights are about the same as the light bars through 2019 and taller than the light bars from 2022 to 2024. Year labels in black beneath the bars. Legend at top left: light blue square labelled Orders (M), dark navy square labelled Net Revenue ($B). A small dark rounded-square four-piece logo mark sits in the top right corner of the header, in dark purple/black on purple.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF. White slide with purple header bar (#7B189F) and pale lavender title band (#FAF2FF). Header title in white bold; headline in purple #7B189F bold. Typeface is Poppins-style geometric sans-serif; chart labels in Roboto-style sans-serif. Header title and headline are about 4% of slide height. No shadows or rounded corners apart from the logo.

Combo chart on a white background, years 2015 to 2024 on the x-axis with no axis lines or gridlines. The chart fills the rest of the slide, with the legend at the upper left, and a footnote at the bottom centre-left and a slide number at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Column chart of 12 steel-blue bars (Last 12 Month SOTG&A, excluding stock-based comp, USD millions), one per quarter from Q1'22 to Q4'24. There is no y-axis and no gridlines. Value labels in blue-grey sit above each bar, and quarter labels in black sit below. Values: Q1'22 $1,832; Q2'22 $1,992; Q3'22 $2,116; Q4'22 $2,143; Q1'23 $2,102; Q2'23 $2,008; Q3'23 $1,924; Q4'23 $1,863; Q1'24 $1,795; Q2'24 $1,721; Q3'24 $1,650; Q4'24 $1,595. A small dark four-lobed rosette logo sits at the top right of the purple header, and a tiny dot is at the lower right of the chart.

There are no shadows or rounded corners; the bars have square corners. Title and headline are about 3.95% of slide height; legend about 2.7%; footnote about 1.5% in italic.

### Slide 46

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Lays out profitability as three labeled stages on a single timeline, moving from breakeven to mid-single-digit to double-digit margin, which makes the progression easy to follow.
- Review: The deck has a clear four-part route (Home category, advantages, growth, financials) with consistent purple branding, divider slides, and action-style titles, and it closes on a margin roadmap that the audience can follow (slides 46 and 47).
- Worth keeping: Keep the section structure, the purple title bar system, the badge-lift flow on slide 16, and the three-step roadmap on slide 46.

**Layout**

Purple header bar with a white four-lobed rounded cross/diamond logo at the top right. A thin purple horizontal timeline line runs through three circular nodes at y about 45%. Node 1 (x about 16%) has a light lavender fill, purple outline and a dark charcoal checkmark. Node 2 (x about 49%) has a pale fill, purple outline and a dark checkmark. Node 3 (x about 83%) is an empty pale circle with a purple outline and no checkmark. Three solid purple rectangular tags hold the Step labels. Three beige rectangular cards with no outline sit below the dark band.

**Design**

Beige cards #F6F0F0 with charcoal text #363038. Typeface is Poppins (geometric sans-serif) in semibold/bold weights throughout. Title about 4% of slide height, step headings about 3.7%, band headings about 3.5%, card text about 2.7%, footnote about 1.4% italic. Flat shapes, no shadows or gradients, square corners.

### Slide 47

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Breaks the path to 10%+ margin into stacked contribution bars by driver, so the audience can see where each margin point is expected to come from.
- Review: The deck has a clear four-part route (Home category, advantages, growth, financials) with consistent purple branding, divider slides, and action-style titles, and it closes on a margin roadmap that the audience can follow (slides 46 and 47).
- Review: Slide 47 has a floating gross margin bar with no value, a crowded text panel, and a waterfall that does not reconcile visibly, which undercuts the closing page.
- Suggested repair: Fix the overlap on slide 14, give the gross margin bar on slide 47 a label and a value, and enlarge the data labels and legends on slides 42 to 45.

**Text**

Path to 10%+ Adjusted EBITDA¹ Margins
How do we get to 10%+ Adjusted EBITDA¹ margins?
OpEx
2-4%
Marketing
1-2%
Other
1-2%
Logistics
1-2%

**Layout**

Full-width purple header bar with left-aligned title and a corner logo at the right. The left ~75% holds a subtitle question and a waterfall chart with labels beneath. A pale lavender sidebar on the right (25% width) holds a heading and paragraph, left-aligned. Footnote runs along the bottom left and the page number sits at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Waterfall chart without axes, on a thin light grey baseline at about y 84%. Left dark purple bar (Mid Single Digit Adjusted EBITDA Margin) rises from the baseline. Five floating medium purple steps ascend left to right: Advertising 2-3%, Logistics 1-2%, Other 1-2%, then a pale lilac floating bar for Gross Margin Contribution (no label inside), then Marketing 1-2% and OpEx 2-4%. The final tall dark purple bar (10%+ Adjusted EBITDA Margin) rises from the baseline to the OpEx top level. Each step has a bold dark-grey category label above its box. A small four-piece rounded dark logo icon is in the top-right corner of the header.

Background: White #FFFFFF with purple header band #7B189F and right panel #FAF2FF. ### Slide 49

**Text**

Non-GAAP / Financial / Measures
To supplement our consolidated financial statements presented in accordance with generally accepted accounting principles (“GAAP”), this presentation contains certain non-GAAP financial measures, including Adjusted EBITDA, Adjusted EBITDA Margin Free Cash Flow and Free Cash Flow Margin. We use these non-GAAP financial measures internally in analyzing our financial results and believe they are useful to investors, as a supplement to GAAP measures, in evaluating our ongoing operational performance. We have provided a reconciliation of certain of these non-GAAP financial measures to the most directly comparable GAAP financial measure in the appendix to this presentation.
We calculate Adjusted EBITDA as net income or loss before depreciation and amortization, equity-based compensation and related taxes, interest income or expense, net, other income or expense, net, provision or benefit for income taxes, net, non-recurring items and other items not indicative of our ongoing operating performance. Adjusted EBITDA Margin is calculated by dividing Adjusted EBITDA by Net Revenue. We have included Adjusted EBITDA and Adjusted EBITDA Margin because they are key measures used by our management and board of directors to evaluate our operating performance, generate future operating plans and make strategic decisions regarding the allocation of capital. In particular, we believe the exclusion of certain expenses in calculating Adjusted EBITDA facilitates operating performance comparisons on a period-to-period basis as these costs may vary independent of business performance. For instance, we exclude the impact of equity-based compensation and related taxes as we do not consider this item to be indicative of our core operating performance. Investors should, however, understand that equity-based compensation and related taxes will be a significant recurring expense in our business and an important part of the compensation provided to our employees. Accordingly, we believe that Adjusted EBITDA and Adjusted EBITDA Margin provide useful information to investors and others in understanding and evaluating our operating results in the same manner as our management and board of directors.
We calculate Free Cash Flow as net cash provided by or used in operating activities less net cash used to purchase property and equipment and site and software development costs (collectively, “Capital Expenditures”). Free Cash Flow Margin is calculated by dividing Free Cash Flow by Net Revenue. We believe Free Cash Flow and Free Cash Flow Margin are important indicators of our business performance as they measure the amount of cash we generate. Accordingly, we believe that Free Cash Flow and Free Cash Flow Margin provide useful information to investors and others in understanding and evaluating our operating results in the same manner as our management.
We calculate forward-looking non-GAAP financial measures based on internal forecasts that omit certain amounts that would be included in forward-looking GAAP financial measures. We do not attempt to provide a reconciliation of forward-looking non-GAAP financial measures to forward looking GAAP financial measures because forecasting the timing or amount of items that have not yet occurred and are out of our control is inherently uncertain and unavailable without unreasonable efforts. Further, we believe that such reconciliations would imply a degree of precision and certainty that could be confusing to investors. Such items could have a substantial impact on GAAP measures of financial performance.
The non-GAAP financial measures have limitations as analytical tools. We do not, nor do we suggest that investors should consider such non-GAAP financial measures in isolation from, or as a substitute for, financial information prepared in accordance with GAAP. Investors should also note that the non-GAAP financial measures we use may not be the same non-GAAP financial measures and may not be calculated in the same manner as that of other companies, including other companies in our industry.

- text at x 29.2, y 30.7, w 66.2, h 1.9 [font000000002d946b66; font size 1.85; #414141]: measures used by our management and board of directors to evaluate our operating performance, generate future operating
- text at x 29.2, y 33.0, w 67.2, h 1.9 [font000000002d946b66; font size 1.85; #414141]: plans and make strategic decisions regarding the allocation of capital. In particular, we believe the exclusion of certain expenses
- text at x 29.2, y 35.2, w 66.2, h 1.9 [font000000002d946b66; font size 1.85; #414141]: in calculating Adjusted EBITDA facilitates operating performance comparisons on a period-to-period basis as these costs may
- text at x 29.2, y 37.4, w 67.9, h 1.9 [font000000002d946b66; font size 1.85; #414141]: vary independent of business performance. For instance, we exclude the impact of equity-based compensation and related taxes

- text at x 29.2, y 41.8, w 67.8, h 1.9 [font000000002d946b66; font size 1.85; #414141]: equity-based compensation and related taxes will be a significant recurring expense in our business and an important part of the
- text at x 29.2, y 44.1, w 64.9, h 1.9 [font000000002d946b66; font size 1.85; #414141]: compensation provided to our employees. Accordingly, we believe that Adjusted EBITDA and Adjusted EBITDA Margin provide
- text at x 29.2, y 46.3, w 64.8, h 1.9 [font000000002d946b66; font size 1.85; #414141]: useful information to investors and others in understanding and evaluating our operating results in the same manner as our

- text at x 29.2, y 53.0, w 67.2, h 1.9 [font000000002d946b66; font size 1.85; #414141]: We calculate Free Cash Flow as net cash provided by or used in operating activities less net cash used to purchase property and
- text at x 29.2, y 55.2, w 67.6, h 1.9 [font000000002d946b66; font size 1.85; #414141]: equipment and site and software development costs (collectively, “Capital Expenditures”). Free Cash Flow Margin is calculated by
- text at x 29.2, y 57.4, w 65.7, h 1.9 [font000000002d946b66; font size 1.85; #414141]: dividing Free Cash Flow by Net Revenue. We believe Free Cash Flow and Free Cash Flow Margin are important indicators of our
- text at x 29.2, y 59.6, w 66.4, h 1.9 [font000000002d946b66; font size 1.85; #414141]: business performance as they measure the amount of cash we generate. Accordingly, we believe that Free Cash Flow and Free
